To our knowledge, this is the first evidence that long-timescale Earth-observation embeddings can support short-timescale weather downscaling where sub-grid departures are systematically structured by persistent surface properties.

</details>


### [94] [A Framework for Designing Reward Functions: From Objectives to Features to Human-Aligned Reward Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12302)
*Di Yang Shi,W. Bradley Knox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形式化流程，使非专家能够基于自然语言任务描述，通过三步（提炼目标并推导结果变量、因果子集选择奖励项、偏好询问拟合权重）实例化并迭代人类对齐的线性奖励函数，其中权重拟合被建模为凸可行性问题，通过分离预言机以O(n log κ)次偏好查询收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励函数设计通常需要专家知识和大量人工调试，非专家难以直接实例化符合人类偏好的奖励函数。现有方法缺乏形式化的保证，尤其是在权重可行域的无冲突性和查询效率方面存在不足。

Method: 论文提出三步式形式化流程：(1) 引导式工作流，从自然语言任务描述中提炼基本目标并推导可测量的结果变量；(2) 将奖励项选择归约为因果DAG上的最小成本部分覆盖问题，通过最大流算法在多项式时间内求解；(3) 将权重拟合建模为凸可行性问题，通过偏好查询迭代收窄可行域，并利用分离预言机求解，保证可行权重区域确定性无冲突，并以O(n log κ)次偏好查询收敛至期望容差。

Result: 该方法首次实现了确定性无冲突的可行权重区域维护，并通过分离预言机以O(n log κ)次偏好查询将可行域收窄到期望容差，这是首个具备该理论保证的奖励设计方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为奖励函数设计提供了一条从自然语言任务描述到人类对齐线性奖励函数的完整形式化路径，兼具理论保证和实用性，适合非专家用户使用。

Abstract: We present a formal process to enable non-experts to instantiate and iterate on human-aligned reward functions, i.e. reward functions that adhere to a given preference ordering over trajectories. Given a task described in natural language, our process produces a linear reward function in three steps: distill the task's objectives into a set of fundamental objectives and derive measurable outcome variables that capture those fundamental objectives, select a causally representative subset of outcome variables as the reward terms, and fit weights to those reward terms via preference elicitation. Our contributions describe the first step and formalize the latter two steps. The first is a guided workflow for deriving outcome variables. The second is a reduction of reward term selection to minimum-cost partial cover on a causal DAG, solved in polynomial time via max-flow. The third is a geometric framing of weight fitting as a convex feasibility problem iteratively narrowed by preference queries, solved by existing separation oracle methods. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first reward-design method that maintains a deterministically conflict-free feasible weight region, narrowed to a desired tolerance via a separation oracle with O(n log κ) preference queries.

</details>


### [95] [Redistribution-based Cost Inference Improves Sparse Safe Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12306)
*Ebenezer Gelo,Geraud Nangue Tasse,Steven James,Benjamin Rosman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RCI框架，将轨迹级二值停止反馈通过收益分解转化为稠密的逐步骤成本，用于安全离线强化学习中的安全约束策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全离线RL假设可获得密集的逐步骤成本标注，但实际中监督者通常只提供轨迹级的停止反馈（在第一个不安全转移时的二值信号），缺乏逐步骤归因，这构成了时序信用分配难题。

Method: 提出基于重分布的代价推断（RCI）框架：通过收益分解将稀疏的停止反馈转换为稠密的逐步骤成本，然后在增强数据集上训练带约束的离线策略。并证明收益等价的再分布保留了可行策略集和CMDP中最优拉格朗日乘子。

Result: 在高速公路驾驶和机器人操作实验中，相比稀疏基线和方法器基线，RCI显著降低了违反约束率，并展现出对异构数据集组成和标签噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 理论上转换是无损的（保留可行策略集和最优拉格朗日），实践中改善了成本批评器学习的条件，为实际场景下的安全离线强化学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Safe offline RL typically assumes access to dense per-step cost annotations, but in practice supervisors provide only trajectory-level stop-feedback: a binary signal at the first unsafe transition, with no per-step attribution. We frame this as a temporal credit assignment problem and propose the Redistribution-based Cost Inference (RCI) framework, which converts sparse stop-feedback into dense per-step costs via return decomposition, then trains a constrained offline policy on the augmented dataset. We show that return-equivalent redistribution preserves the feasible policy set and the optimal Lagrangian in a CMDP, establishing that the transformation is lossless in theory while yielding better-conditioned cost critic learning in practice. Experiments on highway driving and robotic manipulation demonstrate substantially lower violation rates than sparse and classifier-based baselines, with robustness to heterogeneous dataset compositions and label noise.

</details>


### [96] [AI4AI at Test-Time: Strong-to-Weak Capability Transfer via Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12307)
*Cheng Qian,Wenting Zhao,Liangwei Yang,Heng Wang,Jielin Qiu,Heng Ji,Silvio Savarese,Huan Wang,Shelby Heinecke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种测试时知识迁移方法——强到弱脚手架（strong-to-weak scaffolding），即由更强的builder模型构建推理时脚手架来帮助较弱的target模型，无需任何参数更新即可将任务表现从0.49提升到0.91，效果接近翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的模型蒸馏需要在训练时更新参数（如teacher forcing、on-policy distillation），本文想探索是否可以在测试时实现能力的迁移，而不需要对较弱模型做任何参数更新。

Method: 使用4个Theory-of-Mind基准任务，builder模型利用5%的数据作为验证集，通过多轮迭代精炼推理脚手架，最终在完整测试集上评估。脚手架的核心机制包括将不稳定的模型推理卸载为确定性代码、基准特有的路由和严格的答案格式约束。

Result: 测试时能力迁移非常有效，目标模型平均性能从0.49提升到0.91（接近翻倍）。收益主要来自将不稳定推理卸载到确定性代码、基准特有路由和严格答案格式执行，而非鼓励更深入的推理或更广泛的采样。Builder模型的推理努力单调提升脚手架质量，较弱目标模型获得最大增益。

Conclusion: 推理时脚手架设计是传统训练时蒸馏的重要补充，使强模型无需重新训练即可将认知结构迁移到弱模型，开辟了测试时知识迁移的新方向。

Abstract: Recent work on distillation transfers the capabilities of large models to smaller ones often by updating the latter's parameters, through teacher forcing, on-policy distillation, and related training-time methods. In this paper, we ask whether such transfer can instead occur at test time. We study strong-to-weak scaffolding: whether a stronger builder model can construct inference-time harnesses that help a weaker target model solve tasks more reliably without any parameter updates. Using four representative Theory-of-Mind benchmarks, each builder model uses 5% of the data as a validation set to iteratively refine its harness over multiple rounds, after which the finalized harness is evaluated on the full test set. Empirically, this form of test-time capability transfer is highly effective, nearly doubling average target-model performance from 0.49 to 0.91. Our analysis shows that the gains come primarily from offloading unstable model reasoning into deterministic code, benchmark-specific routing, and strict answer-format enforcement, rather than from encouraging the target model to reason more extensively or sample more broadly. We further find that builder-model reasoning effort improves harness quality monotonically, platform effects are modest relative to the builder model's own capability, and weaker target models receive the largest gains. These results suggest that inference-time harness design is an important complement to conventional training-time distillation, enabling strong models to transfer cognitive structure to weaker models without retraining.

</details>

### [97] [Socioduality: A Relational Process Framework for Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11322)
*Mehmed Zahid Çögenli*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"社会二重性"（socioduality）这一过程性构念，用于刻画人机交互中双方贡献如何通过序列、互惠且携带历史的互动过程形成，并提供了可操作的编码协议与初步验证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HCI/人机交互研究多关注个体能力、联合表现或最终产出，但无法保留下交互中一方回应如何成为另一方下一步贡献形成条件的过程信息。

Method: 定义社会二重性构念，采用嵌套分析单元（动作、确认的社会二重性片段、关联路径、交互容器）；规定最小片段A1-B1-A2需要回应偶然性与回报偶然性证据；分三类（确认、非社会二重性、不确定）分类候选片段后进行二级编码；提出三个命题；采用冻结操作协议，通过两轮独立模型评估器对三个未见过的人机对话记录进行校准。

Result: 动作与候选片段重建在两个案例中完全一致，第三个案例仅在一个局部多模态单元化决策上有差异；剩余分歧集中在回报偶然性边界处。协议展示了可复现性和可操作性。

Conclusion: 社会二重性提供了一个有界且经验上可处理的交互过程构念，能保留端点中心分析无法恢复的路径信息，为分析人机贡献如何通过互动形成提供了新工具。

Abstract: Human-AI research often evaluates individual capabilities, combined performance, or final outputs, but these approaches do not preserve how one party's response becomes part of the conditions under which the other party's next contribution is formed. This article introduces socioduality, a sequential, reciprocal, and history-carrying relational process between two distinguishable parties in which a response from one party becomes part of the observable conditions under which the other party's subsequent contribution, judgement, decision, or action is formed. Specified for human-AI dyads, the construct uses nested units: moves, confirmed sociodual episodes, linked pathways, and the broader interaction container. A minimum episode A1-B1-A2 requires evidence of response contingency and return contingency; candidate episodes are classified as confirmed, non-sociodual, or indeterminate before secondary coding of response orientation and substantive contribution re-formation. Three propositions address history-conditioned formation, pathway divergence, and robustness differences among endpoint-equivalent pathways. A frozen operational protocol was calibrated on three previously unseen natural human-AI records through two separately executed model-based evaluator series. Move and candidate reconstruction converged exactly in two cases and differed by one local multimodal unitisation decision in the third; remaining disagreement was concentrated at return-contingency boundaries. Socioduality therefore provides a bounded and empirically tractable process construct for analysing how human and AI contributions are formed through interaction while preserving pathway information that endpoint-centred analysis cannot recover.

</details>


### [98] [QUARTZ: Qualitative Understanding via Accessible Representation and Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11364)
*Omar Khan,JooYoung Seo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了QUARTZ系统，一个为盲人和低视力（BLV）研究人员提供屏幕阅读器可访问的定性数据可视化多模态表示的网络系统。通过RITE方法与8名BLV参与者进行用户研究，完成了跨四种可视化类型的12项任务，揭示了定性可视化特有的无障碍访问障碍（非线性导航中断和语义理解差距），并记录了迭代式协同设计如何解决这些问题，最终贡献了实证证据和设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定性数据可视化（如概念图、网络图、桑基图和编码条纹）在研究中不可或缺，但对BLV研究人员完全不可访问。现有的多模态无障碍解决方案仅针对定量图表，定性可视化的非线性、语义丰富的结构从未被关注，导致BLV研究人员无法独立参与定性研究。

Method: 开发了基于Web的QUARTZ系统，提供屏幕阅读器可访问的多模态定性数据可视化表示；采用Rapid Iterative Testing and Evaluation（RITE）方法，与8名BLV参与者开展用户研究，完成12项跨四种可视化类型的任务；通过迭代式协同设计（co-design）不断优化系统并解决无障碍问题。

Result: 研究发现了定性可视化独有的无障碍访问障碍，包括非线性导航中断和语义理解差距，这些在定量图表研究中不存在；同时记录了迭代式协同设计如何有效解决这些障碍，并产出了实证证据和设计指南。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了一个未被探索的可视化领域的无障碍访问空白，推进了BLV研究人员独立参与定性研究所需的基础设施建设，为该领域的后续工作提供了可复用的设计指导。

Abstract: Qualitative data visualizations -- concept maps, network graphs, Sankey diagrams, and coding stripes -- are integral to research practice, yet remain entirely inaccessible to blind and low-vision (BLV) researchers. While visualization has seen advanced multimodal solutions for quantitative charts, qualitative visualizations, and their non-linear, semantically rich structures have received no attention. We present QUARTZ, a web-based system that provides screen-reader-accessible, multimodal representations of qualitative data visualizations. Using the Rapid Iterative Testing and Evaluation (RITE) method, we conducted a user study with 8 BLV participants who completed 12 tasks across four visualization types. Our findings expose accessibility barriers unique to qualitative visualizations -- non-linear navigation breakdowns and semantic comprehension gaps absent from quantitative chart research---and document how iterative co-design with BLV users resolved them. We contribute empirical evidence and design guidelines for an underexplored visualization domain, advancing the infrastructure BLV researchers need to participate independently in qualitative inquiry.

</details>


### [99] ["I Don't Want My Mental Health App To Give Me Mental Health Barriers": Unpacking The Need For Digital Mental Health Tracking Services With And For The Blind Community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11391)
*Omar Khan,JooYoung Seo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过93名法定盲人成年人的问卷调查和10人的半结构化访谈，揭示了数字心理健康（DMH）追踪服务对盲人用户的系统性排斥模式，指出其功能界面设计以视力正常用户为前提，并提出了透明化预购买无障碍评估、原生无障碍界面设计和用户控制的数据代理权等设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字心理健康追踪服务承诺提供持续个性化的健康支持，但设计往往默认面向视力正常用户，导致盲人社区面临独特的排斥模式——无法在付费前评估可访问性、社区功能排除目标用户、用户虽具备数字素养却无法实际使用。研究旨在系统理解盲人用户在这些服务中的经历，以推动包容性设计。

Method: 采用解释性序贯混合方法研究设计：第一阶段对93名法定盲人成年人进行问卷调查（了解使用模式、采纳决定和数据代理偏好）；第二阶段对10名受访者进行半结构化访谈。定量数据使用描述性统计和Kruskal-Wallis检验分析，开放题和访谈数据使用归纳主题分析，并通过Norman和Skinner的电子健康素养框架进行解释。

Result: 参与者最常使用的类别为正念、睡眠和目标追踪服务，但普遍反映被排除在其他用户最重视的社区支持功能之外。研究发现"计算机素养"维度单独不足以解释排斥现象：许多参与者具备素养，却因先于用户存在（predate the user）的设计选择而无法应用其素养。

Conclusion: 研究贡献了面向DMH追踪服务的设计建议：透明的预购买可访问性评估、原生无障碍（而非事后改造）的界面设计、用户控制的数据代理权。这些建议的目标不是简单地"容纳"盲人用户，而是从一开始就与盲人用户共同设计DMH追踪服务。

Abstract: Digital mental health (DMH) tracking services promise continuous, personalized support for well-being, but their design often assumes sighted users. For the blind community, this assumption produces a distinct pattern of exclusion: services whose accessibility cannot be evaluated without first paying for them, community features that exclude the users they purport to support, and interfaces that leave users digitally literate but functionally blocked. We report on an explanatory sequential mixed-methods study of blind users' experiences with DMH tracking services in the United States. In the first phase, 93 legally blind adults completed a survey about their usage patterns, adoption decisions, and data-agency preferences; in the second, 10 survey respondents participated in semi-structured interviews. We analyzed closed-ended responses using descriptive statistics and the Kruskal-Wallis test, and open-ended and interview data using inductive thematic analysis, interpreting findings through Norman and Skinner's eHealth Literacy framework. Participants identified mindfulness, sleep, and goal-tracking services as their most-used categories, but also described recurring exclusion from the community-support features that other users value most. We argue that the framework's "computer literacy" dimension is insufficient on its own: many of our participants possessed the literacy but were blocked from applying it by design choices that predate the user. We contribute design recommendations for transparent pre-purchase accessibility evaluation, accessibility-native rather than retrofitted interfaces, and user-controlled data agency -- recommendations intended not to accommodate blind users but to design DMH tracking services with them from the start.

</details>


### [100] [The Role of Variability in Human-Machine Interaction Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11401)
*Sean Kille,Jan Lars Hagemann,Anne Voormann,Balint Varga,Andrea Kiesel,Sören Hohmann*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑人类行为自然变异性的最优控制器，并与传统降低变异性的共享控制方式及纯人类控制进行对比实验，结果表明该变异性感知控制器在保持任务表现的同时显著提升了人机交互的可用性感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共享控制与触觉辅助方法忽略了人类运动行为的随机特性，这可能限制人机交互的性能和体验质量，因此需要一种能够保留人类自然运动变异性的控制策略。

Method: 设计了一个物理耦合的触觉交互实验，在三种控制模式下（传统降变异控制器、变异性感知控制器、纯人类控制基线）进行对比，测量行为变异性、任务表现和交互体验。

Result: 变异性感知控制器在维持任务表现的同时显著提升了感知交互质量（可用性方面）。

Conclusion: 将人类随机运动特征纳入共享控制设计具有重要意义，所提出的控制策略为人机交互中以人为中心的设计提供了可行且有效的方法。

Abstract: Human-machine interaction (HMI) requires control strategies that account for the nature of human motor behavior. Conventional shared-control and haptic-assistance methods typically ignore the stochastic nature of human behavior, potentially limiting both performance and human interaction experience. In this study, we designed an experimental setting and evaluated a novel human-variability-aware optimal controller. Participants performed a physically coupled haptic interaction task in three conditions: a controller mode that aims at conventionally reducing overall variability, a variability-aware controller mode designed to maintain human natural variability patterns, and a human-only control condition serving as a baseline. We analyzed behavioral variability, task performance, and human interaction experience. The results show that considering natural movement variability significantly increased perceived interaction quality in terms of usability while maintaining task performance. These findings highlight the importance of incorporating stochastic human movement characteristics into shared-control designs and demonstrate the feasibility and benefits of the proposed control strategy for human-centered control design of HMI.

</details>


### [101] [How Children Collaborate within Programmable AR Environments with Co-Located Collaborative Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11442)
*Romina Mahinpei,Diya Ajay Hundiwala,Sandy Zhang,Lana Glisic,Andrés Monroy-Hernández*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究扩展了儿童可编程增强现实（AR）应用Capybara，加入同地协作功能（支持多设备间的共享可见性和交互），并通过9名儿童的工作坊实验考察协作如何产生。研究发现协作通常是轻量级和隐性的，表现为三种互补形式：带社交意识的平行游戏、迭代式混改和自发同伴支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程AR环境有望支持儿童的创造性学习，且AR系统日益支持同地协作体验，但关于儿童如何在具有同地协作功能的可编程AR环境中进行协作，目前知之甚少。

Method: 扩展现有儿童可编程AR应用Capybara，加入支持跨设备共享可见性和交互的同地协作功能；随后组织9名儿童参与工作坊，观察和分析协作是否以及如何产生。

Result: 协作在实践中多为轻量级和隐性的，主要通过三种互补形式涌现：带着社交意识的平行游戏、迭代式混改、以及自发的同伴支持。

Conclusion: 研究结果为设计未来以儿童为中心的、更好支持同地协作体验的可编程AR系统提供了设计见解。

Abstract: Programmable augmented reality (AR) environments are emerging as a promising way to support children's creative learning through embodied interaction with digital characters and physical space. At the same time, AR systems are increasingly capable of supporting co-located collaborative experiences. However, little is known about how children collaborate within programmable AR environments offering co-located collaborative features. In response, we extended Capybara, an existing programmable AR application for children, with co-located collaborative features supporting shared visibility and interaction across devices. We then conducted workshops with 9 children to examine whether and how collaboration emerges during use. Across our workshops, collaboration was often lightweight and implicit, emerging through three complementary forms: parallel play with social awareness, iterative remixing, and spontaneous peer support. Together, our findings provide insights for designing future child-centered programmable AR systems that better support co-located collaborative experiences.

</details>


### [102] [Player Perceptions of Generative AI in Games: A Steam Review Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11539)
*Mahsa Bazzaz,Seth Cooper*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过定量与定性混合方法，研究了Steam平台上游戏玩家对生成式AI内容的态度，发现披露使用生成式AI的游戏推荐率和情感倾向显著低于使用程序化内容生成（PCG）的游戏，玩家认为生成式AI代表开发者投入不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在游戏开发中快速普及并引发大量玩家讨论，但缺乏实证研究系统考察玩家对AI生成内容的真实感知，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 采用混合方法：(1)定量分析Steam市场中PCG与生成式AI游戏的采纳情况；(2)定性分析508,192条英文评论，并对其中的600条进行主题分析。

Result: 披露使用生成式AI的游戏推荐率更低、整体情感更负面；玩家将生成式AI的使用感知为开发者对游戏投入不足的表现。

Conclusion: 基于人本AI框架，成功的生成式AI采纳应服务于玩家需求而非仅为降低开发成本。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of generative AI in game development has created large discussions among players, yet little empirical work has examined how players actually perceive AI-generated content. Employing quantitative methods, we study the adoption of generative AI in games in the Steam marketplace, using procedural content generation (PCG) as a baseline of a generative technology that was successfully integrated into games over several decades. Furthermore, using qualitative methods, we study player reception of generative AI by analyzing 508,192 English-language reviews. We found that games disclosing generative AI use receive lower recommendation rates and more negative overall sentiment than PCG games. Thematic analysis of 600 reviews shows that players perceive the use of generative AI in games as low developer investment in the game. Drawing on human-centered AI frameworks, we argue that successful generative AI adoption requires deploying generative AI for what players need, not for what makes development cheaper.

</details>


### [103] [Measuring Browser Webcam Gaze Honestly: A Capture-Clock Methodology and Open Reference Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11566)
*Chi-Sheng Chen,Gabriel A. Brat*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文指出浏览器网页摄像头眼动追踪器报告的延迟数据存在系统性偏差，因为常见做法是在眼动样本发出时而非源帧捕获时打时间戳，导致测量的推理延迟几乎为0ms。作者提出利用rVFC API恢复逐帧捕获时钟，实现诚实的延迟测量，并提供开源TypeScript实现和基准测试工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浏览器端基于摄像头的眼动追踪器被越来越多地用于大规模数据采集和临床应用，但报告的延迟数字不能反映真实性能。现有测量方法在发射眼动样本时打时间戳，而非源帧捕获时，掩盖了引擎真实的速度，需要一种诚实的延迟测量方法。

Method: 从浏览器的requestVideoFrameCallback (rVFC) API恢复逐帧捕获时钟（优先使用captureTime，否则用presentationTime作为可验证的下界），通过逐帧队列实现精确的源帧配对（适用于暴露推理管线的引擎），对不暴露管线的引擎（如WebGazer）则提供进一步的下界估计。发布开源TypeScript实现和基准测试工具。

Result: 在WebGazer和一个新的FaceMesh+KRR管线上展示了所提出的诚实延迟测量方法和基准测试工具，证明了该方法能够揭示真实延迟，区别于传统测量方法得到的表面上的0ms延迟。

Conclusion: 浏览器端眼动追踪的延迟测量需要基于源帧捕获时间而非样本发出时间，才能真实反映系统性能；提出的方法为不同透明度的引擎提供了精确测量或可验证的下界估计方案。

Abstract: Browser-based webcam gaze trackers are increasingly used for crowd-scale data collection and in clinical settings where lab eye trackers are impractical, but the reported latency numbers may not represent real world functionality. The common practice of timestamping each gaze sample when it is emitted, rather than when its source frame was captured, makes the measured inference latency read about $0\,$ms no matter how slow the engine really is. We show how to measure it honestly, recovering a per-frame capture clock from the browser's \texttt{re\-quest\-Video\-Frame\-Call\-back} (rVFC) API (\texttt{captureTime} where the browser exposes it for local camera streams, else \texttt{presentationTime}, in which case every recovered latency is a verifiable lower bound): exact source-frame pairing through a per-frame queue for engines that expose their inference pipeline, and a further lower bound for engines that do not, such as WebGazer. We release an open TypeScript implementation and benchmark harness, demonstrated on two interchangeable engines: WebGazer and a new FaceMesh+KRR pipeline.

</details>


### [104] [RAGE-Vis:A Relation-Aware Generative Editing Interface for Natural Language-Based Chart Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11581)
*Ziyao Kang,Yiping Sun,Linxuan Tian,Henghuan Qu,Wei Zeng,Jiazhi Xia*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出RAGE-Vis，一个基于关系感知的生成式编辑系统，支持用户通过自然语言对位图图表进行跨组件的复合编辑。系统将图表图像转换为可编辑的参数化中间表示，解析复合意图并生成分层编辑面板，同时根据视觉编码关系、结构关系和表达一致性关系识别受影响的字段，实现跨组件的协调控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表编辑方法通常将自然语言指令映射到单一操作或控件，难以处理高层级、跨组件的复合请求，且因缺乏对图表组件间关系的感知，常产生局部合理但全局不一致的结果。

Method: RAGE-Vis的流程包括：(1) 将位图图表图像转换为可编辑的参数化中间表示；(2) 解析用户的复合编辑意图，识别目标与作用范围；(3) 为不确定性请求生成分层编辑面板，支持全局设置和局部参数调整；(4) 基于视觉编码关系、结构关系和表达一致性关系识别受影响字段，并组织为可操作的控件以支持跨组件协调编辑。

Result: 通过两个案例研究展示了RAGE-Vis在样式调整、数据扩展、顺序重排、图例布局和颜色映射等复杂编辑任务中的适用性；用户研究进一步表明参与者能够有效处理不完全指定的请求、探索候选方案，并借助RAGE-Vis维持跨组件的一致性。

Conclusion: RAGE-Vis通过关系感知的生成式编辑框架，有效解决了自然语言图表编辑中复合意图解析和跨组件一致性的关键问题，为自然语言驱动的图表编辑提供了新的交互范式。

Abstract: Natural language offers an easy way for users to express chart editing intents, which are often composite and cross-component (e.g., adjusting style, extending categories, highlighting values). However, existing methods typically map instructions to a single operation or widget, limiting their ability to handle high-level requests and often producing locally plausible but globally inconsistent results due to a lack of awareness of relationships between chart components. To address these challenges, we introduce RAGE-Vis, a Relation-Aware Generative Editing interface for natural language-based chart editing. The system supports bitmap chart images as input and converts them into an editable parameterized intermediate representation. Instead of mapping instructions to a single edit or widget, RAGE-Vis parses composite intents, identifies targets and scopes, and generates hierarchical editing panels for underspecified requests, enabling users to adjust both global settings and local parameters. Furthermore, RAGE-Vis identifies potentially affected fields based on visual encoding relations, structural relationships, and expressive consistency relations, and organizes them into actionable widgets to support cross-component coordinated controls. Through two case studies, we demonstrate the applicability of RAGE-Vis in complex editing tasks, including style adjustment, data extension, order rearrangement, legend layout, and color mapping. A user study further shows that participants can effectively handle underspecified requests, explore candidate alternatives, and maintain cross-component consistency with RAGE-Vis.

</details>


### [105] [VisPuzzle: Task-Aware Composite Visualization Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11635)
*Zheng Wang,Zhiyang Shen,Lingyun Yu,Shixia Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VisPuzzle 提出了一种任务感知的可视化组合方法，将组合问题建模为组合图上的逐步搜索问题，并使用蒙特卡洛图搜索高效生成高质量的组合候选方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将多个可视化组合成连贯整体仍然具有挑战性，因为设计空间巨大，且需要平衡任务相关数据洞察的覆盖、感知清晰度和美学质量。

Method: 将可视化组合形式化为组合图上的逐步搜索问题。图中节点表示数据组合操作（如并集、连接）或视觉组合操作（决定组件关系、空间排列、组件比例），边编码操作之间的可行转换。采用蒙特卡洛图搜索，并由平衡任务相关性、感知有效性和美学连贯性的奖励函数引导。

Result: 用例和用户研究表明，VisPuzzle 生成的排名靠前的组合候选与人类对组合质量的判断高度一致。

Conclusion: VisPuzzle 在支持有原则且可扩展的可视化组合方面展现出实用性。

Abstract: Compositing multiple visualizations into a coherent whole remains challenging due to the vast design space and the need to balance the coverage of task-relevant data insights (e.g., trends and outliers), perceptual clarity, and aesthetic quality. In this paper, we present VisPuzzle, a task-aware method that formulates visualization composition as a stepwise search problem over a composition graph. In this graph, nodes represent either data composition operations (e.g., union, join) or visual composition operations that determine component relationships, spatial arrangements, or component proportions, and edges encode feasible transitions between operations. We employ Monte Carlo Graph Search to efficiently identify high-quality composition candidates from this graph, guided by a reward function that balances task relevance, perceptual effectiveness, and aesthetic coherence. A use case and a user study show that the top-ranked candidates produced by VisPuzzle align closely with human judgments of composition quality, demonstrating its utility in supporting principled and scalable visualization composition.

</details>


### [106] [A Browser-Based Gesture-Driven Avatar Interaction Framework for Metaverse Onboarding Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11708)
*Deepti Parachuri,Chhayank Sahu,Sameer Singh Choudhary*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于浏览器的元宇宙入职环境中构建的手势驱动交互层，用户通过手、手臂和头部手势（而非键盘或控制器）在虚拟房间中探索并与视频、文档和测验内容交互。系统结合Google MediaPipe实时手势识别和两种移动技术（举手导航和原地行走），在五个参与者的内部入职会话中进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了让元宇宙中的化身交互更加自然和沉浸，需要摆脱控制器式界面，使用户能够直接通过身体手势与虚拟环境互动，但目前缺乏轻量级、可Web部署的无控制器交互模型的集成与评估。

Method: 构建了一个手势驱动的浏览器端交互层，使用Google MediaPipe进行实时手势识别，并集成了两种替代移动方式：举手导航和原地行走，用户可根据任务在精确性和身体沉浸感之间进行权衡。通过五个参与者的结构化内部入职会话进行评估。

Result: 报告了在组合这些技术于单一部署系统时的实际效果（哪些有效、哪些无效）以及设计中出现的权衡取舍。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是将这些技术作为一个统一的、轻量级、可Web部署、无需控制器的交互模型进行集成、部署和评估，并从中提炼出实用的设计经验和权衡。

Abstract: Avatar interaction shapes how engaging and immersive a metaverse experience feels, and for that interaction to feel natural, avatars need to respond to users without forcing them through a controller-based interface first. This paper describes a gesture-driven interaction layer built for a browser-based metaverse onboarding environment, where users explore a set of virtual rooms as an avatar and interact with embedded video, document, and quiz content using hand, arm, and head gestures instead of a keyboard or controller. The system combines real-time gesture recognition (Google MediaPipe) with two alternative locomotion techniques - hand-raise navigation and in-place walking - so users can trade off precision against physical immersion depending on the task. The contribution is the integration, deployment, and evaluation of these techniques as a single lightweight, web-deployable, controller-free interaction model, assessed through a structured internal onboarding session with five participants. We report what worked, what didn't, and the design trade-offs that came out of combining these techniques in one deployed system.

</details>


### [107] [ATOM: Geometry-Aware Microgesture towards Object-Agnostic Tangible Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11871)
*Yinqiao Wang,Hao Xu,Qixuan Liu,Shengdong Zhao,Pheng-Ann Heng,Chi-Wing Fu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出ATOM框架，通过指尖感知检测和几何增强技术，将日常手持物体转化为支持0D、1D、2D微手势交互的有形用户界面，在跨物体场景下优于消融基线，并展示了良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是支持跨不同日常物体的微手势交互，并自动利用每个物体的几何可供性，实现物体无关的有形交互体验。

Method: 采用指尖感知检测流程，结合生成式2D和3D模型进行几何增强与细化；引入基于可用性的方法，根据人体工程学适宜性对检测元素进行优先级排序；在此基础上开发AR系统，将日常手持物体转化为支持0D、1D、2D微手势交互的有形用户界面。

Result: 在与不同形状和尺寸的日常烹饪物体交互中，ATOM在任务完成率、可用性（SUS）和工作负载（NASA-TLX）方面均优于消融基线；对10个物体的进一步研究验证了ATOM跨物体和抓握方式的泛化能力。

Conclusion: ATOM展示了在真实AR场景中实现流畅、物体无关的有形交互的潜力，为日常物体转变为可交互界面提供了有效框架。

Abstract: This paper presents ATOM, an integrated framework towards agnostic and tangible object interactions with microgestures. Our goal is to support microgesture interactions across different everyday objects, with the capability to automatically leverage the geometric affordance of each object. We formulate a fingertip-aware detection pipeline to leverage generative 2D and 3D models for geometry enhancement and refinement. We then introduce a usability-based method to prioritize the detected elements based on their ergonomic suitability for interactions. Building on this foundation, we further develop an AR system to transform everyday handheld objects into tangible user interfaces with 0D, 1D, and 2D microgesture interactions. Across transitions among everyday cooking objects of varying shapes and sizes, ATOM outperformed ablation baselines in task completion, usability (SUS), and workload (NASA-TLX). A further study with 10 objects demonstrates ATOM's generalizability across objects and grasps, highlighting its potential towards fluid, object-agnostic tangible interaction in real-world AR scenarios.

</details>


### [108] [Synchronized AMG and EMG Dataset of Lower-limb Muscle Activities in Everyday Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11958)
*Dongxu Tang,Shih Ying-Lei,Zhuoyi Ren,Jianting Liao,Yitian Shao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个同步多模态数据集SAME-Limb，用于健康成年人下肢活动研究。数据集包含30名受试者、1918个试验、16种任务条件，记录了16个三轴加速度计（AMG）、4通道表面肌电（EMG）和15标记光学动作捕捉（MoCap）数据。通过AMG数据估计关节角度，基准模型平均绝对误差为8.840°-9.591°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解下肢肌群协调对于研究运动障碍、康复和体能表现至关重要，而可复现的分析需要结合局部肌肉信号（EMG）与身体级运动学（MoCap）的多模态记录。现有数据缺乏同步的多模态下肢活动数据集，且AMG作为一种补充神经电信号的机械性肌肉监测方法尚未得到充分利用。

Method: 在左腿16个三轴加速度计分为四个肌肉部位簇进行AMG记录，配合4通道表面EMG；15标记MoCap系统捕获下肢运动学并计算双侧膝踝关节角度。数据集包含30名受试者1918个试验、16种任务条件。使用5-100Hz带通滤波的AMG数据300ms窗口估计四个关节角度，并进行模态消融实验，考察传感器配置、模态、训练受试者数量及频率表示的影响。

Result: 在跨受试者基准测试中，四个参考模型的平均绝对误差为8.840°-9.591°。基准和消融结果表征了不同受试者、任务和关节角度下的性能，并揭示了传感器配置、模态选择、训练数据规模和频率表示对性能的影响。

Conclusion: 该数据集提供了包含AMG、EMG和MoCap的同步多模态资源，包含详细的时序定义、处理数据和可复现的基准工具，为下肢运动分析和康复研究提供了有价值的公开资源。

Abstract: Understanding how lower-limb muscle groups coordinate is important for studying movement impairment, rehabilitation, and physical performance. Reproducible analysis of this coordination requires multimodal recordings that relate local muscle-related signals with body-level kinematics. Complementing neural-level electrical activation captured by EMG, AMG provides a valuable mechanical approach to monitoring muscle activity. Here, we introduce a synchronized, multimodal dataset for healthy-adult lower-limb activities. For data collection on the left leg, 16 triaxial accelerometers were evenly divided into four muscle-site clusters for AMG recording, complemented by four surface EMG channels. A 15-marker optical motion-capture (MoCap) system captured lower-body kinematics, with the resulting marker trajectories used to compute bilateral knee and ankle joint angles. Our dataset contains 1,918 trials from 30 subjects across 16 task conditions. We benchmark the dataset by estimating four joint angles from 300 ms windows of the 5-100 Hz band-pass-filtered AMG data and assess matched EMG features in a separate modality ablation. In the primary cross subject benchmark, the four reference models achieved mean absolute errors of 8.840$^\circ$-9.591$^\circ$. The benchmark and ablation results characterize performance across subjects, tasks, and joint angles and examine the effects of sensor configuration, modality, the number of training subjects, and frequency representation. The release includes documented timing definitions, processed data, and reproducible benchmark resources. https://dongxutang918-afk.github.io/SAME-Limb/

</details>


### [109] [IF:CARGO: LLM-Based Semantic Compilation for Al-Native Rule Programming Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12195)
*Ting-Chen Hsu,Lianye Zhang,Jiangxu Lin,Zhaoyi Yu,Fei Qin,Zihao Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一款实验性解谜游戏IF: CARGO，将大语言模型作为语义编译器而非自主游戏代理。玩家用自然语言编写IF/THEN规则，模型将其翻译为受限命令模式供游戏引擎确定性执行。通过对24名参与者的混合方法测试，证明了这种"语义调试"交互模式的有效性，并提出了AI原生游戏设计的实用模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型在游戏中的新型应用方式，挑战将LLM作为自主游戏代理的传统思路，转而将其定位为语义翻译中介，实现玩家表达与确定性执行之间的平衡。

Method: 构建IF: CARGO实验性解谜游戏，采用自然语言IF/THEN规则输入、LLM语义编译、游戏引擎确定性验证与执行的架构，并通过24名参与者的8个关卡混合方法playtest评估玩家尝试次数、思考时间、可操控性感知和策略调整等指标。

Result: 玩家普遍能够将模型理解为翻译中介，并能通过反馈调整策略；但周期性命令、多机器人协调和规则优先级机制带来更高的认知与诊断负担。

Conclusion: 提出AI原生游戏设计的三种实用模式：约束自然语言输入、保留玩家作者身份、确保确定性执行，将AI交互框架化为"语义调试"过程。

Abstract: This case study presents IF: CARGO, an experimental puzzle game that uses a large language model as a semantic compiler rather than an autonomous game-playing agent. Players author IF/THEN rules in natural language, which the model translates into a constrained command schema for deterministic validation and execution by the game engine. This architecture creates a playable loop of expression, execution, observation, and revision, framing AI interaction as semantic debugging. A mixed-methods playtest with 24 participants across eight levels examined player attempts, thinking time, perceived controllability, adjustability, and interpretations of the AI's role. Results suggest that players generally understood the model as a translation intermediary and could revise their strategies through feedback, while periodic commands, multi-robot coordination, and rule-priority mechanics created greater cognitive and diagnostic demands. The study proposes a practical pattern for AI-native gameplay: constrain natural-language input, preserve player authorship, and ensure deterministic execution.

</details>


### [110] ["Pharos Night: Crown Pursuit": An AI-Native Deck-Building and Tactical Arena Game Design Based on Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12216)
*Ting-Chen Hsu,Jueyao Liu,Yanzi Zhou,Jiangxu Lin,Haoyu Xu,Yuwen Liu,Yanjia Liu,Bangjing Xu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了"Pharos Night: Crown Pursuit"——一个基于多智能体系统的AI原生卡牌构筑与战术竞技游戏。游戏利用大语言模型生成材料和卡牌、支持NPC决策及自然语言交互，并通过JSON结构化解析、预定义机制映射等约束手段确保生成结果可控。13名参与者的小规模试玩表明该系统能提供具有战略深度和吸引力的AI驱动玩法，但也暴露出可预测性、透明度和玩家控制方面的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI技术的发展，越来越多的研究者开始探索由生成式AI直接驱动游戏规则的"AI原生游戏"，本文旨在探索如何利用多智能体系统和大语言模型构建具有涌现性的AI原生游戏体验。

Method: 设计并实现了一个基于多智能体系统的AI原生卡牌构筑与战术竞技游戏，采用大语言模型生成游戏材料和卡牌、支持NPC决策及自然语言交互；通过将模型输出解析为结构化JSON、从预定义机制构建卡牌效果、将定性效果等级映射为设计师指定的数值等手段来约束模型生成结果。

Result: 13名参与者的小规模试玩结果表明，该系统能够提供具有战略意义和吸引力的AI驱动游戏体验，同时也揭示了可预测性、透明度和玩家控制方面的挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了多智能体生成式AI系统在创造更具涌现性的数字游戏体验方面的潜力，同时指出了当前AI原生游戏在可控性与玩家体验之间需要进一步平衡的问题。

Abstract: With advancements in generative AI technology, an increasing number of researchers have begun exploring AI-native games in which gameplay rules are directly driven by generative AI. This paper presents "Pharos Night: Crown Pursuit," an AI-native deck-building and tactical arena game based on a multi-agent system. The game uses large language models to generate materials and cards, support NPC decision-making, and mediate natural-language interactions. During play, players collect materials, describe desired card effects in natural language, and choose whether to negotiate or fight with NPCs in the arena. To constrain model-generated outcomes, the system parses responses as structured JSON, constructs card effects from predefined mechanics, and maps qualitative effect levels to designer-specified numerical values. A small-scale playtest with 13 participants suggests that the system can provide strategically meaningful and engaging AI-driven gameplay, while also revealing challenges related to predictability, transparency, and player control. This work demonstrates the potential of multi-agent generative AI systems for creating more emergent digital game experiences.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Dynamic Governance of Multi-LLM Agent Systems for Collaborative Conversational Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11207)
*Alexander Liss,Nicholas Desmond,Santiago Gil Gallego*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体多轮对话中因缺乏共享目标函数导致的交互崩塌问题，提出一种控制理论驱动的治理层——Experience Orchestrator (EO)。EO通过上下文老虎机、PID控制器和POMDP信念追踪器三个机制，在6万次模拟中使高意图顾问联系率提升32个百分点（78.1% vs 46.1%），且CB变体选择解释了97%的结果方差，证明治理策略而非环境初始条件决定对话轨迹终点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个目标结构对立的LLM智能体在多轮交互中，由于缺乏共享目标函数，会出现"崩塌"现象：访客投降、站点智能体停止策略变化、对话无法达成任何一方目标。作者希望探究控制理论治理层能否替代缺失的目标函数来解决这一问题。

Method: 在模拟金融服务环境中构建Experience Orchestrator (EO)，包含三个机制：1) Contextual Bandit (CB)根据真实网络分析数据选择内容变体；2) PID控制器通过动态模式约束强制执行行为一致性；3) POMDP信念追踪器维护访客意图的概率模型。通过6万次模拟实验验证。

Result: EO相比朴素LLM控制实现+32个百分点的提升（78.1% vs 46.1%）；CB变体选择解释了97%的因素间结果方差；人物层面分析揭示了两个不同的行为模式：对无自然转化倾向的访客，治理层决定系统功能与否；对已接近对齐的访客，朴素LLM的共情默认值已足够。

Conclusion: 治理策略比环境初始条件更能决定对话轨迹的最终结果。但所有发现均基于LLM-LLM模拟，PID控制器尚未针对真实人类不可预测性校准，在真实流量上验证EO是关键的下一步工作。

Abstract: When two LLM agents with structurally opposed objectives interact across multiple turns, the absence of a shared goal function produces not competition but collapse: the visitor capitulates, the site agent stops varying its approach, and the conversation terminates without achieving either agent's stated objective. This paper asks whether a control-theoretic governance layer can substitute for that missing goal function. The Experience Orchestrator (EO) addresses this in a simulated financial services environment where a site agent guides a visitor toward advisor contact while the visitor maintains psychologically realistic resistance. EO governs the joint trajectory through three mechanisms: a Contextual Bandit (CB) that selects content arms calibrated from real-world web analytics, a PID controller that enforces behavioral consistency via dynamic schema constraints, and a POMDP belief tracker that maintains a probabilistic model of visitor intent. Across 60,000 simulations, EO achieves a +32 percentage point lift in high-intent advisor contact rate (78.1% vs. 46.1% over a naive LLM control), with CB variant selection accounting for 97% of between-factor outcome variance -- confirming that the governance policy, not environmental initial conditions, determines where trajectories end up. Persona-level analysis reveals two distinct regimes: for visitors with no natural inclination toward conversion, the governance layer is the difference between a functional system and a non-functional one; for visitors already near alignment, a naive LLM's empathetic defaults are largely sufficient. All findings are conditional on LLM-to-LLM simulation. The PID controller has not been calibrated against real human unpredictability, and validating EO on live traffic is the critical next step.

</details>


### [112] [Distribird: Literature-Informed Prior Distribution Design for Bayesian Model Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11210)
*Patrik P. Süli,György Eigner,Roland Hollós*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Distribird，一个智能体驱动的Web应用，用于自动化贝叶斯校准中的先验分布构建。它通过多智能体管线搜索文献、提取数值并按领域相关性加权、拟合概率分布（AIC模型选择），在无文献时回退到合理的无信息先验，并明确报告证据来源和置信度。评估显示其先验质量与单提示LLM基线相当，但在可追溯性、有效性和隐私保护方面显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于过程的贝叶斯模型校准需要为每个参数设定先验分布，但研究者几乎总是使用均匀先验，因为从科学文献构建信息性先验既慢又需要领域和统计双重专业知识。这一痛点阻碍了贝叶斯方法的实际应用。

Method: Distribird采用多智能体管线：根据参数名、物理描述和领域上下文搜索文献，提取文献中报告的值并按领域相关性加权，通过AIC模型选择拟合概率分布。无文献时回退到合理的无信息替代方案。所有LLM调用在本地运行，仅生成的搜索词发送到公共文献数据库。评估使用3个开源模型（Qwen3.6 27B、Gemma 4 31B、Mistral Small 4 119B）与单提示LLM基线对比，测试了10个科学领域的24个参数。

Result: 在先验质量上，完整的Distribird管线与单提示LLM基线持平。但Distribird的每一个先验都可追溯到具体的论文和数值；内置有效性层对超出范围请求拒绝生成先验（而基线在30个模型-参数案例中有11个返回了自信但无根据的先验）；所有语言模型调用均在本地运行，保护了未发表的建模细节不被传输给第三方LLM提供商。

Conclusion: 对于科学用途，可追溯性、有效性和隐私保护这些属性比点估计精度的边际提升更为重要。Distribird在保证先验质量的同时，解决了文献构建先验的自动化、可验证性和数据安全问题。

Abstract: Bayesian calibration of process-based models requires a prior distribution for each model parameter. Despite decades of methodological work, researchers almost always fall back on uniform priors. The main reason is that building informative priors from scientific literature is slow and needs both domain and statistical expertise. We present \textbf{Distribird}, an agentic web application that automates this process. Given a parameter name, physical description, and domain context, Distribird deploys a multi-agent pipeline that searches the literature, extracts and weights reported values by domain relevance, and fits a probability distribution via AIC model selection. When no literature is available, the system falls back to sensible uninformative alternatives, and clearly reports both the evidence behind and the confidence level of every prior it produces. It is designed for the problems where the models have physically interpretable parameters, where domain knowledge exists in the published literature. We evaluate the tool on 24~parameters across 10 scientific domains comparing three open-weight models (Qwen3.6 27B, Gemma 4 31B, Mistral Small 4 119B) with a single-prompt LLM baseline. On prior quality the full pipeline \emph{matches} this baseline. Every prior is traced to the specific papers and values from which it was constructed; a built-in validity layer declines to produce priors for out-of-scope requests, whereas the single-prompt baseline returns confident but unfounded priors for them in 11 of 30~model--parameter cases; and every language-model call runs locally, so no parameter description or unpublished modelling detail is transmitted to a third-party LLM provider (only generated search terms reach the public literature databases). For scientific use, we argue these properties matter more than a marginal improvement in point-estimate accuracy.

</details>


### [113] [A Forced-Structure Reduction and Verifiable Bounds for Conway's 99-Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11211)
*Aalok Thakkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报道了自主AI研究代理对Conway 99-图问题（是否存在srg(99,14,1,2)）的系统性攻击。主要贡献包括：证明循环图在Z/99上最多满足68.0%的约束条件；通过强制结构约简将问题转化为84顶点上的12-正则图；提出并验证了有自同构轨道存在性框架；最终获得69.43%的最佳验证工件，并给出证据表明这是鲁棒的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conway's 99-graph problem是组合数学中著名的开放问题，即判定参数为srg(99,14,1,2)的强正则图是否存在。该问题长期未解，本文尝试用自主AI研究代理进行系统性、可复现的攻击，以推动对该问题的理解。

Method: 本文采用四种验证性方法：(1) 穷举证明循环图约束满足上限；(2) 强制结构约简：利用λ=1和μ=2的条件将存在性问题约简为12-正则84顶点图，并用CP-SAT编码验证；(3) 建立预定自同构轨道存在性框架（无不动点和单不动点作用），用已知图验证；(4) 多方法搜索最佳部分解工件。

Result: 循环图约束上限为3366/4950=68.0%（33个差异类）；通过约简验证了唯一srg(9,4,1,2)的恢复；自同构框架在srg(9,4,1,2)和Paley图srg(13,6,2,3)上得到验证；最佳工件达到69.43%，且十四种不同方法均未超过该值，表明这是鲁棒边界。

Conclusion: 该工作虽未直接证明或否定srg(99,14,1,2)的存在性，但提供了多层次的精确边界和可复现的验证框架。任何低于4950的可证明上界都将构成非存在性证明，因此69.43%的边界与原始开放问题紧密相关，为后续研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Conway's 99-graph problem asks whether a strongly regular graph with parameters $\mathrm{srg}(99,14,1,2)$ exists. We report a systematic, fully reproducible attack by an autonomous AI research agent, scored under the track's partial-credit metric. Our verifiable contributions are: (1) an exhaustive proof that no circulant graph on $\mathbb{Z}/99$ satisfies more than $3366/4950=68.0\%$ of the constraints ($33$ of $49$ difference-classes), with the same ceiling for the other abelian group of order $99$; (2) a forced-structure reduction: $λ=1$ makes each neighbourhood a perfect matching and $μ=2$ puts the outer vertices in bijection with non-matched neighbour-pairs, collapsing existence to a $12$-regular graph on $84$ vertices, encoded for CP-SAT and validated by recovering the unique $\mathrm{srg}(9,4,1,2)$; (3) a validated prescribed-automorphism orbit-existence framework (fixed-point-free and single-fixed-point actions, checked on $\mathrm{srg}(9,4,1,2)$ and the Paley graph $\mathrm{srg}(13,6,2,3)$), and (4) a best verified artifact at $69.43\%$, with evidence that this is a robust frontier (fourteen distinct methods, none exceeding it) entangled with the open question, since any provable bound below $4950$ is a non-existence proof.

</details>


### [114] [Detecting a Route Flip Is Easier Than Knowing Whether to Fix It: Causal Route-Mediated Damage in Quantized Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11212)
*Parvel Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究Top-k混合专家（MoE）路由在4-bit KV缓存量化扰动下的行为。研究发现约三分之一的量化损伤由路由介导（RMF≈0.31），虽然可部署的路由器裕度能检测到token跨决策边界翻转（AUC 0.772），但无法区分翻转的有害性或有益性——在测试的局部推理可观测统计量中，不存在能预测翻转损失符号的指标（运气水平），构成经验性的效益检测障碍。该结论跨三种架构稳健，但真实int4 KV内核实验的置信区间包含零，只能排除严重分歧而不能独立复现。所有假设、阈值和评估均在测量前预注册。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-k MoE路由是不连续的，部署场景中数值扰动（如4-bit KV缓存量化）可能使token跨越决策边界，导致专家选择翻转。此前缺乏对这类路由介导损伤的系统性因果归因和检测能力边界研究。

Method: 提出四轮实验装置：1）定价路由介导分数（RMF）；2）token级归因分解机制；3）预注册探针跨三种架构验证；4）受控同检查点flag交换实验。另含真实int4 KV内核对照测试。

Result: OLMoE-1B-7B上RMF≈0.31；路由裕度检测翻转的AUC为0.772但无法区分好坏；无任何局部统计量能预测损伤符号（运气水平）；跨架构迁移成立；真实内核CI [-0.111, 0.394]包含零，排除粗差异但未独立复现。

Conclusion: 路由介导损伤是可量化且跨架构存在的现象，但存在经验性效益检测障碍：当前推理可观测路由特征无法选择性修复有害翻转。门控归一化约定是损伤幅度调节因子而非路由恢复机制。该领域需要更丰富的特征族或新的方法突破。

Abstract: Top-k Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routing is discontinuous, so a deployment-motivated numerical disturbance -- simulated 4-bit KV-cache quantization read by a protected BF16 gate -- pushes tokens across decision boundaries and flips which experts fire. This paper proposes no new mitigation; it supplies a causal apparatus, empirical findings, and a detection-limit result. A four-run apparatus prices the route-mediated fraction (RMF) of quantization damage, a token-level attribution decomposes it by mechanism, and pre-registered probes carry the findings across three architectures. On OLMoE-1B-7B at 4-bit KV (pilot), about a third of the damage is routing-mediated: RMF ~ 0.31 (discovery 0.31 [0.20, 0.41]; process-replicated mean 0.313 +/- 0.020; pre-registered re-execution 0.231). The deployable router margin detects that a flip occurred (AUC 0.772) but cannot tell a harmful flip from a helpful one (at chance): among the tested local, inference-observable router statistics we find no predictor of a flip's loss sign above chance -- an empirical benefit-detection barrier bounding selective repair restricted to this feature family. The signed-flip tax and sign-inseparability carry cross-model; the clean-reference remedy's payout is architecture-modulated; a controlled same-checkpoint flag-swap re-scopes the gate's normalization convention to a damage-magnitude moderator, not a route-recoverability mechanism. A real int4 KV kernel yields a fraction compatible with the fake-quant dose curve but underpowered (95% CI [-0.111, 0.394] includes zero) -- ruling out gross disagreement, not an independent replication. Hypotheses, thresholds, and evaluations were pre-registered before measurement, with misses reported; a pre-registered held-out read replicates the partition and the near-cancelling tax out of sample, while the strict impossibility exclusion narrowly misses.

</details>


### [115] [Poor Man's Agentic Modeling: Simulating Large LLM-Agent Societies on a Laptop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11215)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用低参数替代模型替换LLM智能体来模拟大规模社会系统的方法，通过"交互阶数×记忆"分类法预测替代误差随智能体数量N的变化趋势，并在多个LLM仿真（如EconAgent）上验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM多智能体社会模拟计算成本高昂，而研究者关心的通常是宏观现象（相变、典型化事实、N的标度行为）而非单个智能体的认知细节。作者希望利用统计物理的洞察，建立一种低成本且可靠的替代方法。

Method: 用从数百到数千次廉价查询拟合出的低参数模型替代每个LLM智能体，然后在笔记本上以任意N运行社会模拟。引入[交互阶数×记忆]分类法，将智能体的感知与记忆映射到有效理论和替代误差的预测N趋势。

Result: 在EconAgent及另外7个具名LLM仿真的忠实复现上验证，智能体决策克隆自真实LLM诱导（主要为DeepSeek），成本仅数美元。预测的误差趋势逐格成立；两个被否证的预测（均针对强饱和响应，归因于其曲率）也被无自由参数的理论定量匹配。

Conclusion: 该方法在模拟运行前即可判断替代是否有效，主要取决于每个智能体感知的内容。统计物理视角下的替代智能体方法可在极大降低计算成本的同时保持宏观模拟的可靠性。

Abstract: Simulating societies of many large language model (LLM) agents is expensive, yet the questions asked of such simulations are usually macroscopic: phase behaviour, stylised facts, and scaling with the number of agents $N$, not the cognition of any single agent. We turn a statistical-physics observation into a method: replace each LLM agent by a low-parameter model fitted from a few hundred to a few thousand cheap queries, then run the society at any $N$ on a laptop. Whether this works is decided before the simulation runs, chiefly by what each agent perceives. We introduce an [interaction order x memory] taxonomy that maps perception and memory to an effective theory and a predicted $N$-trend of the surrogate error. We validate it on a faithful reimplementation of the LLM macroeconomy EconAgent and seven further named LLM simulations, with agent decisions cloned from genuine LLM elicitations (primarily DeepSeek) for a few dollars; the predicted error trends hold cell by cell, and the two refuted predictions, both on a strongly saturating response and traced to its curvature, are themselves matched quantitatively by the theory with no free parameters.

</details>


### [116] [AutoWorldModel-Bench: A State-Centric Benchmark for Automated World-Model Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11216)
*Marjan Moodi,Xuankang Zhu,Fernando De Mesentier Silva,Harold Chaput,Mohammad Reza Taesiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AutoWorldModel-Bench，一个闭环基准测试平台，用于评估前沿AI编程智能体在开放研究环境中的表现。该基准跨越8个游戏环境，采用统一的结构化状态表示，让智能体在固定计算预算下自主改进世界模型。在64次会话中，Claude Opus 4.6和Codex-5.4在63次有改进，其中91%的成功修改属于研究型改动（如新目标函数、表征、滚动程序或架构变更），而非简单超参数调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界建模是一个尚未定型的领域，当前智能体基准大多偏向"按规格工程化"任务，无法评估AI编码智能体在不确定方向下的自主研究能力。本文旨在提供一个更接近真实研究场景的评估框架，测试智能体的开放研究能力。

Method: 构建AutoWorldModel-Bench闭环基准：提供可用的世界模型起始代码和8个游戏环境，采用统一的结构化状态表示（从游戏中提取ground-truth实体状态并转换为共享张量格式），隔离动力学建模与感知环节，支持快速迭代。智能体在固定计算预算下自主提出并实施改进。

Result: 在64次实验会话中，模型在63次中改善了起始世界模型；91%的成功改动属于非平凡的研究型修改（新目标、表征、滚动程序或架构变化），而非超参数调优。该基准成功区分了研究式改进与工程化调整。

Conclusion: AutoWorldModel-Bench为评估AI编程智能体在开放研究场景中的能力提供了有效平台，表明前沿编码智能体已具备自主执行研究型修改的能力，该基准填补了现有智能体评估体系中研究型任务的空白。

Abstract: World modeling is an unsettled field: architectures, training objectives, and state representations interact in complex ways, and no single recipe dominates across environments. This makes it an ideal testbed for AI coding agents acting as autonomous researchers--a setting in which the improvement direction is not specified in advance, unlike the engineering-to-spec tasks that dominate current agent benchmarks. We introduce AutoWorldModel-Bench, a closed-loop benchmark in which frontier coding agents autonomously improve a provided world-model starter under a fixed compute budget. The benchmark spans eight game environments under a unified structured-state representation--ground-truth entity state extracted from each game and consumed through a shared tensor format--which isolates dynamics modeling from perception and enables minutes-per-run iteration. Across 64 sessions, Codex-5.4 and Claude Opus 4.6 improve their starter on 63; in 91% of sessions the winning edit is a non-trivial research-style modification--a new objective, representation, rollout procedure, or architectural change--rather than a hyperparameter tweak. Our benchmark offers a setting in which frontier coding agents can be evaluated on open-ended research rather than engineering-to-spec problems.

</details>


### [117] [MaSRead: Content-Addressed Reading of Replicated Latent Stores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11218)
*Carlos Baquero,Luís Brito,João Resende*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MaSRead是一种基于内容寻址的读取方法，使智能体能够从复制的潜在空间中按需读取所访问的片段，而非存储全部文本，即使在未知查询到来时也能通过关键词路由和解码实现有效读取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的潜在空间共享机制（如key-value缓存片段）虽然可以通过无冲突复制数据类型实现收敛，但由于共置片段相互干扰，"共置不等于可寻址"，导致后到的查询无法可靠读取已合并的缓存内容。

Method: MaSRead通过不透明键控标签集（由片段词导出）进行路由，配合硬注意力掩码解码每个选中片段以隐藏其他内容。利用词汇连通性，通过图遍历到达多跳查询所需的片段。

Result: MaSRead在链式、流水线、对称、中心和自然语言存储等多种场景中，能隔离恢复访问过的片段，在无关片段累积时仍保持有效，并能迁移到其他模型家族。物化解码的工作量取决于片段长度而非总存储大小。

Conclusion: 复制的潜在存储可以通过内容连通性实现选择性可读，但存在明确局限：词汇路由可能错过不连通的证据，答案组合受限于冻结的阅读器。

Abstract: Independent agents that reason in latent space can share computed state as key-value cache fragments rather than text. Merged by a conflict-free replicated data type, these fragments form a store that converges under any delivery order or duplication. Yet a later query, unknown at encode time, cannot reliably read the merged cache: colocated fragments interfere, so colocation is not addressability. MaSRead addresses the read to content. It routes through opaque keyed tag sets derived from fragment words and decodes each selected fragment under a hard attention mask that hides the rest. Under lexical connectivity, a graph walk reaches the fragments required by a multi-hop query. Across chain, pipeline, symmetric, hub, and natural-language stores, MaSRead recovers visited fragments in isolation, remains effective as unrelated fragments accumulate, and transfers to another model family. After routing, materialized decoding depends on fragment length rather than total store size; end-to-end work still includes store-dependent routing and one read per visited fragment. The limits are explicit: lexical routing can miss disconnected evidence, and answer composition remains bounded by the frozen reader. Thus a replicated latent store becomes selectively readable for later queries when the needed fragments connect to the query through content.

</details>


### [118] [From Monolithic to Modular: Segment-level Automatic Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11219)
*Nikita Kulin,Viktor Zhuravlev,Artur Khairullin,Sergey Muravyov,Ilya Makarov,Daniil Sukhorukov,Ekaterina Averkova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SAPO，一种段级自动提示优化方法，将提示分解为角色、上下文、任务和输出格式四个部分，基于top-5和bottom-5示例进行针对性改进，在多个基准上优于APO基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动提示优化（APO）方法通常对提示进行整体重写，可能导致在改善某一行为的同时损害其他行为。这种"一刀切"的优化方式缺乏对提示内部不同功能模块的精细化处理能力。

Method: SAPO将提示分解为角色、上下文、任务和输出格式四个段级组件，利用top-5和bottom-5示例进行针对性改进。优化循环使用单个LLM配合静态元提示和结构化输出来完成分段、弱点分析和候选生成。整个过程包括两阶段生成：(1)段级诊断和推荐提取，(2)受弱/强段信号约束的候选合成。同时设计了训练/验证协议。

Result: 在SQuADv2、TweetEval、XSUM、CommonGen和GSM8K上，使用GPT-3.5-Turbo和GPT-4o-mini进行评估，SAPO的平均得分优于Zero-shot以及APE、OPRO、EvoPrompt、GEPA和StraGO等强APO基线方法。

Conclusion: 段级提示优化相比整体重写更有效，能够通过细粒度诊断和针对性改进实现更好的提示优化效果。

Abstract: Automatic Prompt Optimization (APO) often rewrites prompts monolithically, which can improve one behavior while degrading others. We present SAPO, a segment-level APO method that decomposes prompts into role, context, tasks, and output format, then applies targeted improvements based on top-5 and bottom-5 examples. The optimization loop uses one LLM with static meta-prompts and structured outputs for segmentation, weakness analysis, and candidate generation. We describe a train/validation protocol and a two-stage generation process: (1) segment-level diagnosis and recommendation extraction, (2) candidate synthesis constrained by weak/strong segment signals. Using the evaluation setup across SQuADv2, TweetEval, XSUM, CommonGen, and GSM8K on GPT-3.5-Turbo and GPT-4o-mini, SAPO achieves the best average score against Zero-shot and strong APO baselines including APE, OPRO, EvoPrompt, GEPA, and StraGO.

</details>


### [119] [LLMs in Process Diagram Engineering: From Optimal PFDs to Validated P&IDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11220)
*Timur Zakarin,Sergei Voitov,Sergei Shumilin,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了P&ID Pilot——一个端到端AI流水线，用于化工流程图的自动生成与转换。该方法结合遗传算法（GA）和大语言模型（LLM）实现PFD合成，再利用LLM智能体将PFD转换为P&ID，最终实现流程设计自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，工艺流程图（PFD）的创建及其向管道仪表图（P&ID）的转换主要依赖人工完成，耗时且成本高昂。引入人工智能有望实现流程自动化、节省时间并获得经济效益。

Method: 采用两阶段流水线：第一阶段通过对比四种方法，选用GA与LLM混合方法合成最优有效的PFD拓扑；第二阶段利用基于LLM的智能体，通过受限工程软件开发工具包生成经过验证的可执行修改，将PFD转换为P&ID。

Result: GA+LLM混合方法在所有方法中获得最低损失值，满足出口流量参数且无工程规则违反；LLM智能体在P&ID转换阶段实现100%执行成功率，同时保持领域规则和参考图结构的一致性。

Conclusion: P&ID Pilot作为统一流水线，通过耦合GA/LLM驱动的合成与LLM转换智能体，为端到端过程设计自动化提供了可行路径，能生成可验证、可部署的输出，显著减少人工工程工作量。

Abstract: Nowadays, the creation of a process flow diagram (PFD) and its subsequent transformation into a piping and instrumentation diagram (P&ID) is predominantly performed manually. Applying artificial intelligence in the task could potentially lead not only to process automation and time savings, but also to financial gains by exploring numerous diagram's topology options and reducing manual labor. This research presents P&ID Pilot - a practical end-to-end AI pipeline capable of handling flowsheet developing for both stages. The first stage focuses on PFD synthesis, whereas the second is directed toward modifying the generated PFD into P&ID. After comparing four different methods, the hybrid approach combining genetic algorithms (GA) and large language models (LLM) is shown to generate the optimal valid PFD topology, achieving the lowest loss value among all the methods, while satisfying the required outlet flow parameters without engineering-rule violations. For the second stage, the proposed LLM-based agent successfully transforms the generated PFD into a source-grounded P&ID by producing validated, executable modifications through a restricted engineering software development kit, achieving 100% execution success while maintaining compliance with domain-specific rules and reference graph structures. This unified pipeline - coupling GA/LLM-driven synthesis with an LLM-based transformation agent - offers a feasible path toward end-to-end process design automation by producing validated, deployable outputs and substantially reduces manual engineering effort.

</details>


### [120] [A Conceptual Framework for Refining Influence Knowledge from Simulation Evidence in Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11221)
*Barbara da Silva Oliveira,Julien Deantoni,Nicolas Ferry*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于"影响（Influences）"新概念的概念框架，用于支持仿真活动（simulation campaigns）的迭代和增量式精化，帮助理解信息物理系统（CPS）中由环境介导的交互行为，并通过Simulink/Gazebo协同仿真中的移动机器人案例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS由多方利益相关者开发，系统行为源于各领域工件与环境之间的交互。虽然仿真和协同仿真已成为分析CPS行为的关键手段，但许多由环境介导的交互（超出直接感知和作动范围）因复杂性、时间或领域经验不足而未被建模，导致仿真结果难以被正确理解和利用。

Method: 提出一个概念框架，引入新概念"影响（Influences）"来支持仿真活动的迭代和增量式精化。该框架旨在逐步深化对系统行为的理解，补充和完善未建模的环境介导交互。

Result: 通过一个使用Simulink/Gazebo协同仿真实现的移动机器人案例研究，展示了所提出方法的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于"影响"概念的概念框架能够有效支持CPS仿真活动的迭代精化，帮助开发者理解和利用仿真结果，弥补环境介导交互建模不足的缺陷。

Abstract: Cyber-physical systems (CPS) are typically developed by multiple stakeholders who produce artefacts tailored to their specific domains of expertise. The behaviour of these systems emerges from the interaction between those artefacts and their operational environment. Simulation and co-simulation have become essential approaches for analysing CPS behaviour and, through simulation campaigns, developers can explore system responses under changing conditions, including interactions with the environment. However, the lack of details and understanding of some environmentmediated interactions (typically the ones beyond direct sensing and actuation), which remain unmodelled due to their complexity, a lack of time, or a lack of domain experience, hinders the proper comprehension and exploitation of simulation results. To address these limitations, we propose a conceptual framework leveraging the novel concept of Influences to support the iterative and incremental refinement of simulation campaigns and deepen the understanding of the system behaviour. We demonstrate the proposed approach through a case study involving a mobile robot implemented using Simulink/Gazebo co-simulation.

</details>


### [121] [Harnessing agent memory to build lifelong AI partners for materials scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11224)
*Siyu Liu,Bo Hu,Beilin Ye,He Cao,David J. Srolovitz,Tongqi Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以持久记忆为核心的终身AI伙伴框架，用于材料科学研究。该框架将科研经验存储为可检查的事实和可执行的技能，支持跨模型的检索、修订和迁移。实验表明，该记忆框架在49个真实材料工具使用问题中将GPT-5.2的任务成功率提高近一倍，在元素固体状态方程计算中显著减少错误，并在材料模拟工作流中大幅降低计算负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料研究依赖于积累的经验——有效的工作脚本、可信的协议、失败计算的警告以及对新旧问题的判断力。然而这些经验通常分散在笔记本、代码仓库、任务日志和个人记忆中，难以跨AI智能体迁移，导致可复现性和知识传递面临挑战。

Method: 提出自演化记忆框架，将科学经验组织为可检查的事实（facts）和可执行的技能（skills），使观察、失败边界、协议和验证检查可以被检索、修订和跨模型迁移。该框架围绕持久记忆设计，而非特定的智能体实现。

Result: 在49个真实材料工具使用问题（含138个可执行子任务）中，记忆使GPT-5.2任务成功率几乎翻倍且无需更新模型参数。在元素固体状态方程计算中，记忆将波函数初始化失败转化为执行前防护，结果从22/1/4改善到25/2/0（正确/部分/错误），避免了92%的重复错误。在13个实际材料模拟工作流中，记忆将聚合痕迹负担减半，工具调用减少超过两倍，同时保持能带、声子、空位和功函数分析结果的物理意义。

Conclusion: 智能体记忆可以成为持久性的科学资产——一种便携、自我改进的材料研究经验记录，能够超越任何单一模型或智能体堆栈的生命周期而长久存续。

Abstract: Materials research advances through accumulated experience - scripts that work, protocols that are trusted, warnings attached to failed calculations or experiments, and judgement that links a new question to an old result. This experience is essential for reproducibility and knowledge transfer, yet it is usually fragmented across notebooks, repositories, job logs and individual memory, and it is rarely portable across artificial-intelligence agents. Here we argue that a lifelong AI partner for materials science can be designed around persistent memory rather than around a particular agent implementation. We introduce a self-evolving memory framework that stores scientific experience as inspectable facts and executable skills, so that observations, failure boundaries, protocols and validation checks can be retrieved, revised and migrated across models. We evaluate the idea in three computational settings that expose different layers of materials-research competence. In 49 real-world materials-tool-use questions comprising 138 executable subtasks, memory nearly doubles GPT-5.2 task success without model-parameter updates. In elemental-solid equation-of-state calculations, memory converts a wavefunction-initialization failure into a pre-execution guardrail, improving outcomes from 22/1/4 to 25/2/0 Correct/Partial/Error and avoiding 92% of repeated errors. In 13 practical material simulation workflows, remembered skills and failure facts halve the aggregate trace burden (tokens) and reduce tool calls by over a factor of two by the third round, while preserving physically meaningful outputs in band-gap, phonon, vacancy and work-function analyses. These results show that agent memory can serve as a durable scientific asset; a portable, self-improving record of materials-research experience that outlives any single model or agent stack.

</details>


### [122] [Identity from the Outside: A Conceptual Framework and Research Program for AI Personality Clones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11225)
*Luc E. Brunet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套用于评估AI"人格克隆"的操作性身份分析框架，区分了三种身份标准（目标保真度、类人通用性、个体独特性），提出六因素身份分解模型，并论证长期判断应采用"气候保真度"（匹配条件分布）而非轨迹保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI人格克隆技术的出现使得传统哲学意义上的"个人身份"问题需要在操作层面重新审视。作者试图绕开意识的难题，通过观察者可评估的"表现的不可区分性"来处理身份问题，旨在为克隆技术提供一个可量化、可实验验证的身份评判框架。

Method: 作者提出一种六项因式分解的身份观测模型（基质、倾向性、记忆、更新动力学、上下文、外生偶然性），采用状态空间形式化表达；将不可区分性定义为1减去评判者的区分优势；通过随机消融估计各系数的局部敏感度；并借助λ演算、线性类型系统和双模拟类比进行理论澄清。区分了产品克隆、个体和"委托代理"（delegate）三种对象。

Result: 论文的核心主张是一个条件性猜想：在给定关于智能体对自身持续性的信息假设以及对其自身利害关系后果假设的前提下，版本化（versionability）倾向于降低长期不可区分性。同时作者将现有实证文献映射到三个身份标准上，提出实验项目规划。

Conclusion: 长期正确的评判标准不是轨迹保真度而是气候保真度——匹配一个人可能回应的条件分布。最好的克隆应与原版的分歧方式，恰好是原版自身可能产生的分歧方式。

Abstract: AI "personality clones" force a re-examination of personal identity in operational terms. Setting aside the hard problem of consciousness, we approach identity through the indiscernibility of manifestations, as assessed by an observer over a duration. We distinguish three criteria that "identity" conflates: fidelity to a target person, generic human-likeness, and individuality. We propose a six-term factorization of observed identity (substrate, dispositions, memory, update dynamics, context, exogenous contingencies), with a state-space formulation. Indiscernibility is defined as one minus a judge's distinguishing advantage, and the factorization's coefficients become local sensitivities estimable by randomized ablation. The central claim is a conditional conjecture: given hypotheses about the agent's information on its own persistence and about consequences bearing on its own stakes, versionability tends to degrade long-horizon indiscernibility. An analogy with lambda-calculus, linear typing, and bisimulation clarifies what linearity does and does not establish. Between product-clone and individual we identify a third object, the delegate: a task-limited, bounded-lifespan partial clone ending in a bandwidth-limited testament. We map the empirical literature onto the three criteria, propose an experimental program, and argue that the correct long-horizon criterion is not trajectory fidelity but climate fidelity: matching the conditional distribution of a person's possible responses. The best clone is the one that diverges from the original as the original would have diverged from itself.

</details>


### [123] [Cutting AI Datacenter Energy with Reinforcement Learning: Measured Power Control of LLM Training from One GPU to the Fleet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11226)
*Eliseo Curcio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统性地刻画了强化学习（GRPO）后训练在GPU上的功耗行为，并提出一种PPO元控制器，通过自适应调整工作负载的生成参数来管理功耗。在7B规模下减少了89.8%的功率限制违规，同时提升18.1%的token输出和26.2%的能效；在72B规模下发现了群组规模执行器因模型分片而失效的问题，并提出了基于生成并发量的替代执行器，实现35.7%的额外输出和87.2%更少的违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型开发中，强化学习后训练占据主导地位，但其在GPU硬件上的功耗行为从未被系统刻画。现有数据中心采用静态上限和反应式节流等对负载不敏感的管理机制，会无差别地降低硬件速度，造成资源浪费和能效损失。

Method: 在7B、14B和72B三种规模（1至4块A100）下，以半秒频率采集超过38万个GRPO训练样本的功耗遥测数据；训练一个PPO元控制器，根据实测功耗自适应调整工作负载自身的生成参数；通过执行器权威度扫描诊断分片场景下的控制失效问题，并重新构建基于生成并发量的控制器。

Result: 在500步7B完整轨迹上，控制器将功率限制违规减少89.8%，token输出增加18.1%，能效（每MWh token数）提高26.2%。72B实时部署中，群组规模执行器因模型分片失去控制力；改用生成并发量执行器后，实时72B推理负载三次复现实验显示：相比静态安全基线增加35.7%输出，预算违规率仅2.27±1.08%，违规次数比非受控运行减少87.2%。在30秒窗口下72B瞬时功耗从23.6%降至1.6%，5分钟窗口降至零；16 GPU组合集群在30秒及更长窗口下零违规，峰值需求为铭牌值的50-56%，表明约两倍超额订阅可行。

Conclusion: 基于工作负载感知的强化学习功耗控制器能够显著提升GPU利用率、降低违规并改善能源效率。研究揭示了执行器选择在分布式训练中的关键作用（占用率vs.并发量原则），并表明在合理功耗测量窗口下，数据中心可对GPU集群进行约两倍的功率超额订阅，同时量化了经济和碳影响，并提出了低成本的运营商试点方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning post-training dominates modern language-model development, yet its power behavior on GPU hardware has not been characterized, and datacenters manage GPU power with workload-blind mechanisms, static caps and reactive throttling, that slow hardware indiscriminately. We instrument GRPO training with half-second power telemetry at 7B, 14B, and 72B scales on one to four A100s (380,000+ samples), and train a PPO meta-controller that adapts the workload's own generation parameters to measured power. Against the full 500-step 7B trace, the controller cuts power-limit violations by 89.8% while increasing token output by 18.1% and energy efficiency by 26.2% (tokens per MWh). Deployed live at 72B, the same controller family yields replicated null results, diagnosed as the group-size actuator losing authority under model sharding. An actuator-authority sweep shows the same parameters applied as generation concurrency retain 17-22% power authority, isolating an occupancy-versus-volume principle; a controller rebuilt on that actuator controls a live 72B rollout-generation workload across three replications: 35.7% more output than a static safe baseline at 2.27 +/- 1.08% budget violations, 87.2% fewer violations than uncontrolled operation, and the best mean throughput and energy per token among constrained controllers, with an adaptive threshold rule matching it in one of three operating conditions. Under realistic measurement windows the original 72B transients fall from 23.6% at half-second resolution to 1.6% at 30 s and zero at 5 min; a composed 16-GPU fleet shows zero violations at 30 s and longer, with peak demand at 50-56% of nameplate. For this fleet mix, roughly twofold oversubscription of nameplate appears feasible, subject to operator validation. We quantify the economic and carbon consequences and specify a low-cost operator pilot.

</details>


### [124] [Forecasting Side Effects of Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11227)
*Chong Yong Ong,Alson Wei Jie Sim,Peixin Zhang,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了激活导向（activation steering）技术在大语言模型中产生的副作用，通过构建67种行为的交叉效应矩阵，发现副作用普遍、结构化且常常不对称，但可以在应用导向前进行预测，为主动安全审计提供了可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活导向虽能有效改变模型行为，但常产生意想不到的副作用，影响其安全部署。目前缺乏对这些副作用进行预测的方法，因此需要系统性研究副作用是否可以在应用之前被预测。

Method: 在三个开源权重语言模型上，对67种行为构建了一个交叉效应矩阵，系统性地衡量每种导向对所有其他行为的影响，分析副作用的模式、结构及其与现有相似度启发式方法的关系。

Result: 发现副作用普遍存在、具有结构化特征且往往不对称，无法用现有的基于相似度的启发式方法解释。副作用的大小主要取决于目标行为本身，而其方向可以从未导向的模型表示中以远高于简单基线的准确率预测。

Conclusion: 激活导向具有系统性且可预测的副作用，这一发现使得主动安全审计和更明智地部署导向干预成为可能。

Abstract: Activation steering modifies a language model by adding a learned direction to its hidden activations, enabling targeted behavioral changes without retraining. While effective, steering often produces unintended side effects on other behaviors, making it difficult to deploy safely. We therefore ask: can these side effects be forecasted before steering is applied? We answer this question by constructing a cross-effect matrix over a taxonomy of 67 behaviors across three open-weight language models. We find that side effects are common, structured, and often asymmetric, revealing interactions that cannot be explained by existing similarity-based heuristics. Despite this complexity, we show that side effects are largely predictable before steering is performed. Their magnitude depends primarily on the target behavior, while their direction can be forecasted from the model's unsteered representations with substantially higher accuracy than simple baselines. Our results demonstrate that activation steering has systematic and forecastable side effects, enabling proactive safety auditing and more informed deployment of steering interventions.

</details>


### [125] [Synchronizing Beliefs with Second-Order Theory-of-Mind in Human-Autonomy Teams (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11229)
*Jack Mirenzi,Henny Admoni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视偏好奖励学习中的教师角色，将教师从被动回答学习者查询的"预言机"转变为主动构建训练课程的"知情教师"。作者将偏好学习视为人-自主系统协作问题，同时建模教师对学习者的认知以及学习者对教师模型的二阶认知，并通过仿真证明知情教师优于学习者主导的选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好奖励学习方法将人类教师视为被动预言机，仅回答学习者生成的查询，这浪费了教师对目标（奖励函数）的先验知识。教师若知道目标，本可以比学习者驱动的采集策略更高效地构建训练样本，且该优势随特征维度增长而扩大。因此，需要重新设计交互框架来利用这一关键优势。

Method: 将偏好学习建模为人-自主系统团队问题，耦合两个行为模型：(1) 教师维护对学习者的模型，以设计信息丰富的课程；(2) 学习者维护对教师模型的二阶模型，并通过发出结构化偏好约束（理解性陈述，understanding statements）使教师的学习者模型保持同步。比较了知情教师与学习者主导选择、交替教师下的模型漂移影响，以及二阶心理理论（ToM-2）陈述与均值信念陈述的效果。

Result: 仿真结果表明：(1) 知情教师优于学习者主导的选择策略；(2) 交替教师导致教师模型漂移，削弱知情教师的优势；(3) 理解性陈述能修复该漂移；(4) 当教师对学习者的估计误差方向集中而非均匀分布时，二阶（ToM-2）陈述优于均值信念陈述。

Conclusion: 偏好学习应从被动查询范式转向主动教学范式，知情教师配合双向模型同步机制能够更高效地对齐机器人与人类意图。理解性陈述作为沟通机制能有效维持教师与学习者之间的模型一致性，尤其在多教师或模型漂移场景中意义重大。

Abstract: Comparative feedback, asking people which of two behaviors they prefer, has become a standard way to align robot and agent behavior with human intent when the reward itself cannot be specified directly. Preference-based reward learning typically casts the human teacher as a passive oracle answering learner-generated queries. We argue this forfeits the teacher's defining advantage: knowledge of the objective. A teacher who knows the target can construct training examples more efficiently than any learner-driven acquisition strategy, an advantage that widens as the reward's feature dimension grows. However, exploiting this advantage requires an accurate model of what the learner currently knows. We therefore recast preference learning as a human-autonomy team problem coupling two behavioral models: the teacher maintains a model of the learner to design an informative curriculum, and the learner maintains a second-order model of the teacher's model, emitting structured preference constraints (understanding statements) that keep the teacher's model of the learner synchronized. In simulation, an informed teacher outperforms learner-led selection; teacher-model drift under alternating teachers erodes this advantage; and understanding statements repair it, with second-order (ToM-2) statements outperforming mean-belief statements when the teacher's error about the learner is concentrated in a particular direction rather than spread evenly.

</details>


### [126] [Mechanist: AI as a Scientific Instrument for Discovering the Mechanisms of Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12036)
*Mengru Wang,Junfeng Fang,Shuofei Qiao,Zhenqian Xu,Haoming Xu,Haoxiong Wang,Shumin Deng,Linyi Yang,Zhixiang Cui,Xin Xu,Yunzhi Yao,Buqiang Xu,Fei Shen,Haozhe Luo,Yunxiang Wei,Ningyu Zhang,Julian McAuley,Tat Seng Chua,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Mechanist，一个基于智能体的自主机制发现系统，利用AI作为科学工具，通过构建约1.3万篇可解释性论文的知识图谱和涵盖43百万篇论文的多学科数据库，以及32种基础方法库，实现从发现模型行为到解释和控制AI模型的自动化研究流程，并在安全风险发现、信念机制理论和模型性能干预等方面取得了显著成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型在各领域取得了显著成功，但其能力背后的机制和潜在风险仍鲜为人知。随着AI开发速度加快且日益自动化，机制探索仍主要依赖人工，导致模型能力与我们理解和控制它们的能力之间的差距不断扩大。

Method: Mechanist是一个智能体系统，将AI用作科学仪器进行自主机制发现。系统构建了包含约1.3万篇论文的可解释性知识图谱，整合了涵盖26个领域、4300万篇论文的多学科数据库，并整理了32种用于机制分析、因果干预和验证的基础方法库。

Result: 与Claude Code和现有AI科学家系统相比，Mechanist能生成更有价值的机制假设并更可靠地执行实验。它成功发现了跨模态安全风险传递的意外现象，构建了信念的机制理论，揭示了模型如何表示世界知识、形成信念和推断他人信念，并将这些洞见转化为改善模型性能和引导DNA序列生成的实用干预措施。

Conclusion: Mechanist展示了从发现模型行为到解释和最终控制AI模型的完整研究进展路径，证明自主机制发现能够有效弥合模型能力与理解控制之间的差距，为AI可解释性和安全性研究提供了新的自动化范式。

Abstract: AI models have achieved remarkable success across diverse domains, yet the mechanisms underlying their capabilities and the risks they may pose remain poorly understood. As AI development becomes faster and increasingly automated, mechanistic exploration remains largely manual, widening the gap between what models can do and our ability to understand and control them. To bridge this gap, we introduce Mechanist, an agentic system that uses AI as a scientific instrument for the autonomous discovery of mechanisms underlying AI intelligence. To support autonomous mechanistic discovery, we construct an interpretability-focused knowledge graph of approximately 13,000 papers and integrate it with a multidisciplinary database of 43 million papers spanning 26 fields. We further curate a library of 32 foundational methods for mechanism analysis, causal intervention, and validation. Compared with Claude Code and existing AI-scientist systems, Mechanist generates more valuable mechanism hypotheses and executes experiments more reliably. Mechanist also demonstrates a progression from discovering model behaviors to explaining and controlling AI models. Specifically, Mechanist first uncovers a counterintuitive safety risk in scientific laboratories, showing that unsafe traits can transfer across modalities through apparently safe training data. Mechanist then develops a mechanism theory of belief, revealing how models represent world knowledge, form beliefs, infer the beliefs of others, and how these mechanisms emerge during pretraining. Finally, Mechanist translates these mechanistic insights into practical interventions that improve model performance across diverse scenarios and steer scientific foundation models toward generating DNA sequences with specified properties.

</details>


### [127] [The Edge-based Contiguous p-median Problem with Connections to Logistics Districting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11230)
*Zeyad Kassem,Adolfo R. Escobedo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出边基连续p-中位问题（ECpM），用于将道路网络划分为给定数量的紧凑且连续的管辖区域。提出了两种二值规划模型（基于割集约束的分支切割算法和基于最短路径约束的求解方法），在拥有超过2700个节点和近3400条边的路网上测试，基于最短路径连续性（SPC）约束的模型相比割集约束的分支切割算法在计算时间上加速最高达17倍。SPC约束还被证明是边基p-中位问题的超有效不等式。此外，论文探讨了ECpM与边基区划（EBD）问题之间的结构联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路网络的区域划分（districting）在实际中具有重要意义，如警察巡逻、垃圾收集、销售区域划分等场景需要将路网划分为紧凑且连续的子区域。现有p-中位问题通常不显式要求连续性约束，而现有连续区划模型在大规模路网上求解困难（如割集约束方法在12小时内无法找到可行解），因此需要更高效的建模与求解方法。

Method: 提出了两种二值规划模型：（1）基于割集约束的模型，需要指数数量的约束来保证连续性，配合分离方案的B&C算法；（2）基于最短路径连续性（SPC）约束的多项式规模模型，可直接用现成求解器求解。两种模型均融入了网络距离度量。论文还证明SPC约束是边基p-中位问题的超有效不等式，并分析ECpM与EBD之间的结构联系。

Result: 在超过2700个节点、近3400条边的路网上进行测试，模型含超过960万个二值变量。基于SPC约束的模型通过标准分支定界求解，相对割集约束的B&C实现获得最高达17倍的加速比。同时，基于割集约束的现有EBD模型在12小时内无法对任何测试实例找到可行解，而基于SPC的EBD模型能够将大多数实例求解至最优。

Conclusion: 基于最短路径连续性（SPC）约束的建模方法在求解大规模道路网络连续区划问题上显著优于传统割集约束方法，具有更高的计算效率。SPC约束作为EpM模型的超有效不等式，为理论分析提供了新见解，同时该方法对扩展问题（如EBD）也具有通用性和实用性。

Abstract: This paper introduces the edge-based contiguous p-median (ECpM) problem to partition the roads in a network into a given number of compact and contiguous territories. Two binary programming models are introduced, both of which incorporate a network distance. The first model requires an exponential number of cut set-based constraints to model contiguity; it is paired with a separation scheme that usually generates only a small number of these constraints, namely, a branch-and-cut (B&C) algorithm. The second model utilizes a polynomial number of shortest-path constraints to model contiguity and can be solved with off-the-shelf solvers. The respective solution approaches are tested on road networks with over 2,700 nodes and close to 3,400 edges, yielding models with over 9.6 million binary variables. Solving the model based on shortest path contiguity (SPC) constraints via standard branch and bound attains speedups in computational time of up to 17x relative to the cut set-based B&C implementation. In addition, the SPC constraints are demonstrated to be supervalid inequalities of the edge-based p-median (EpM) model (i.e., for which contiguity is not explicitly required), meaning that they may cut off integer-feasible solutions and some, but not all, of the optimal solutions of this simpler problem. Finally, the paper explores structural insights and connections between ECpM and the edge-based districting (EBD) problem, which enforces an additional work balance criterion. An existing model that utilizes cut set-based contiguity constraints was unable to find a feasible solution within 12 hours for any of the tested instances, while an SPC-based EBD model was able to solve most of these to optimality.

</details>


### [128] [LinearKV: One Cached State Suffices for Position-Independent Caching in Hybrid LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11231)
*Yirui Liu,Ruoling Qi,Longwen Wang,Xuaner Wu,Jian Chen,Yuxin Jin,Jiawei Shao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LinearKV提出了一种面向混合LLM（线性注意力+全注意力）的无训练位置无关缓存框架，通过"解耦初始化"将K个匹配的局部状态映射为单个初始状态，使线性层和全注意力层都能兼容现有PIC方法。研究发现单个缓存状态即可作为线性层初始化器，而精确组合所有缓存状态的方案（HYPIC）不仅多余，在某些架构上甚至有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有位置无关缓存（PIC）方法专为全注意力模型设计，依赖token索引的KV缓存进行重用、拼接和重组。混合LLM用线性递归替代了大部分注意力层，只暴露固定大小的状态，没有token索引的KV可供拼接或局部修复，因此现有PIC方法无法直接适用。作者希望探索PIC能否惠及混合模型，以及需要什么样的适配方案。

Method: 提出LinearKV框架，核心是"解耦初始化"：线性层将K个匹配的局部状态映射为单个初始状态，全注意力层则照常拼接KV。该方案与现有PIC方法完全兼容，直接复用其token选择和重组逻辑。作者对比了两种初始化策略：单个缓存状态初始化与精确组合所有K个缓存状态（HYPIC的做法）。

Result: 在两个GDN模型上，两种初始化策略性能持平，均能恢复最多92%的完整注意力质量；在Mamba-2模型上，精确组合方案在所有选择器下性能崩溃（EPIC下仅恢复46.6%质量），而单个缓存块初始化器能恢复86.8%。单个状态初始化器也更高效，将TTFT降至完整预填充的0.46倍，而精确组合还需额外5-17%开销。实验在LongBench QA和RULER的8K-32K长度下均验证了结果。

Conclusion: 混合LLM可以有效受益于PIC，但关键在于线性层应采用单个缓存状态的简单初始化，而非追求代数精确的状态组合。简单方案不仅在质量上与复杂方案持平甚至更优，在效率上也显著更优，证明了"足够好"的近似初始化在混合架构中比"精确"初始化更具实用价值。

Abstract: LLM serving is increasingly accelerated by position-independent caching (PIC). Existing PIC methods, however, are built for full-attention models, where a token-indexed KV cache underlies its core operations: matching reusable token chunks, concatenating their KV entries, and selectively recomputing a few tokens to restore cross-chunk context. Hybrid LLMs break these primitives---they replace most attention layers with linear recurrences that expose only a fixed-size state, leaving no token-indexed KV to concatenate or to locally repair. This raises a natural question: can PIC benefit hybrid models, and what would it take? We present LinearKV, a training-free hybrid-PIC framework. Its key insight is a \emph{decoupled initialization}: each linear layer maps its $K$ matched local states to a single initial state, while full-attention layers concatenate their KV as before. LinearKV is therefore compatible with existing PIC methods, reusing their token selection and recomputation as-is. Under this framework, we find that a \emph{single cached state} suffices as the linear layer's initializer.
